Honeycomb master
Honeycomb Cloud MCP Server: Mind State and Runbook, every version (master)
Master copy made Nov 24, 2026, from 35 Google Docs in My Drive, all created Aug 7 to Aug 8, 2026. Every copy is kept here in full, in the order it was created, and each one is followed by exactly what changed from the version before it. No keys or passwords were found in any copy.
The 35 copies are three kinds of document, and each is compared with the previous copy of the same kind:
* Mind State specification (28 copies, versions 2.0.0 to 22.0.0). Each version is a fresh write-up of what that release adds, not a running total, so a large "removed" list usually means the earlier release's write-up was replaced, not that the feature was dropped. No version 7.0.0 copy exists in Drive.
* Short "archived version" notes (5 copies, versions 1.0.0, 2.1.0, 3.0.0, 3.5.0 and 4.0.0), all made within six seconds at 5:32 PM on Aug 7 as a look back at earlier releases.
* Master Operational Runbook (2 copies, versions 4.0.0 and 15.0.0).
Version 4.0.0 of the specification exists twice (copies 5 and 12, six minutes apart); copy 12 adds the one-command bootstrap script. Both are kept in full below.

Full text of this version
HONEYCOMB MIND STATE — CLOUD MCP SERVER ARCHITECTURE
Master System Specification & Cross-Project Knowledge Base Architect: Vinny (Founder & CTO / vinnieboy707)
Version: 2.0.0-production
Classification: Confidential / System Architecture
---

1. EXECUTIVE OVERVIEW & TOPOLOGY
The Honeycomb Mind State Cloud MCP Server is a high-throughput, multi-transport Model Context Protocol (MCP) server engineered to serve as the unified knowledge engine and execution runtime across all active projects, technical domains, and future initiatives.
Designed around a Hexagonal Interconnected Knowledge Mesh ("Honeycomb Topology"), the server decouples domain-specific logic into specialized "Cells" while providing a single API surface for AI models, developer clients, and cloud microservices.
Key Transport Capabilities
1. STDIO Transport: Direct pipe integration for local CLI tools, Cursor, Claude Code, and desktop AI agents.
2. HTTP / SSE Transport: Server-Sent Events endpoint (/mcp & /sse) optimized for low-latency cloud deployments on AWS, Docker, or Kubernetes.
3. Hybrid Execution Mode: Dual listening on HTTP port 8080 while maintaining stdin/stdout streams for local agent orchestration.
---

2. HEXAGONAL MIND STATE NODES (PROJECT KNOWLEDGE BASE)
### 🐝 CELL ALPHA: ArbiBot HFT Arbitrage & Flash Loan Fleet
- System Version: v0.0.7 / Smart Contract V2 (solc 0.8.34)
- Role: Algorithmic cross-DEX/CEX flash loan arbitrage trading platform on Ethereum Mainnet.
- Core Tech Stack:
  - Frontend: React (Vite), TanStack Query v5, Wouter, Recharts, Tailwind CSS, Framer Motion.
  - Backend: Express.js, Drizzle ORM, PostgreSQL, ethers.js v6, Socket.io / SSE live streaming (/api/stream/events).
  - Smart Contracts: ArbitrageFlashLoan.sol & ArbitrageFlashLoanV2.sol (Batch quotes, emergency rescue, profit sweeping, on-chain profit tracking).
- Bot Fleet Architecture:
  - Scanners: Adaptive scanning intervals (200ms - 5s) accelerating on high volatility and dampening on quiet markets.
  - Simulators: 50-iteration Monte Carlo simulation engine computing p5/p95 profit spreads with a 60%+ confidence execution gate.
  - Executors: Nonce management, pending transaction tracking, parallel collision avoidance queue.
  - Flash Loan Engine: Integrated zero-fee provider policy and Aave/Balancer flash loan mechanisms.
- Safety & Verification:
  - Circuit Breaker: Auto-trips on 20 consecutive trade/RPC errors with a 60-second cooldown.

HONEYCOMB MIND STATE — CLOUD MCP SERVER ARCHITECTURE
Master System Specification & Cross-Project Knowledge Base (v2.1.0-universal-ai) Architect: Vinny (Founder & CTO / vinnieboy707)
Version: 2.1.0-universal-ai
Classification: Confidential / System Architecture
---

1. EXECUTIVE OVERVIEW & TOPOLOGY
The Honeycomb Mind State Cloud MCP Server is a high-throughput, multi-transport Model Context Protocol (MCP) server engineered to serve as the unified knowledge engine, execution runtime, and universal AI bridge across all active projects, technical domains, and future initiatives.
Designed around a Hexagonal Interconnected Knowledge Mesh ("Honeycomb Topology"), the server decouples domain-specific logic into specialized "Cells" while providing a single API surface for AI models, developer clients, and cloud microservices.
Key Transport & Integration Capabilities
1. STDIO Transport: Direct pipe integration for local CLI tools, Cursor, Claude Code, and desktop AI agents.
2. HTTP / SSE Transport: Server-Sent Events endpoint (/mcp & /sse) optimized for low-latency cloud deployments on AWS, Docker, or Kubernetes.
3. Universal AI Adapter: Middleware translation layer formatting tools, system prompts, and mind state JSON for OpenAI (GPT-4o), Anthropic (Claude Opus/Sonnet), Google (Gemini Spark/Pro), Ollama (Llama 3/DeepSeek), and LangChain / AutoGen.
4. Pickle Rick C-137 Persona Engine: Dedicated hyper-intelligent, tactical, anti-bureaucratic Rick Sanchez persona for direct technical problem solving.
---

2. HEXAGONAL MIND STATE NODES (PROJECT KNOWLEDGE BASE)
### 🐝 CELL ALPHA: ArbiBot HFT Arbitrage & Flash Loan Fleet
- System Version: v0.0.7 / Smart Contract V2 (solc 0.8.34)
- Role: Algorithmic cross-DEX/CEX flash loan arbitrage trading platform on Ethereum Mainnet.
- Core Tech Stack:
  - Frontend: React (Vite), TanStack Query v5, Wouter, Recharts, Tailwind CSS, Framer Motion.
  - Backend: Express.js, Drizzle ORM, PostgreSQL, ethers.js v6, Socket.io / SSE live streaming (/api/stream/events).
  - Smart Contracts: ArbitrageFlashLoan.sol & ArbitrageFlashLoanV2.sol (Batch quotes, emergency rescue, profit sweeping, on-chain profit tracking).
- Bot Fleet Architecture:
  - Scanners: Adaptive scanning intervals (200ms - 5s) accelerating on high volatility and dampening on quiet markets.
  - Simulators: 50-iteration Monte Carlo simulation engine computing p5/p95 profit spreads with a 60%+ confidence execution gate.
  - Executors: Nonce management, pending transaction tracking, parallel collision avoidance queue.
  - Flash Loan Engine: Integrated zero-fee provider policy and Aave/Balancer flash loan mechanisms.
- Safety & Verification:
  - Circuit Breaker: Auto-trips on 20 consecutive trade/RPC errors with a 60-second cooldown.
  - EIP-1559 Gas Oracle: Real-time base fee history, priority fee estimation, and congestion classification.
  - On-Chain Proof: txHash cryptographic verification required for all trade success logging.

1. EXECUTIVE OVERVIEW & TOPOLOGY
The Honeycomb Mind State Cloud MCP Server (v3.0.0) is a high-throughput, multi-transport Model Context Protocol (MCP) server engineered to serve as the unified knowledge engine, execution runtime, and universal AI bridge across all active projects, technical domains, and future initiatives.
Designed around a Hexagonal Interconnected Knowledge Mesh ("Honeycomb Topology"), the server decouples domain-specific logic into specialized "Cells" while providing a single API surface for AI models, developer clients, and cloud microservices.
Key Integrated Domains (v3.0.0 Update)
1. APEX Sovereign v4 Maximum & Revenue Automator v2: Supreme system prompt, SAM.gov/USASpending federal contract intelligence, and 6 critical codebase audits resolved (Gemini 2.0 Flash models, Firebase Admin auth middleware, server-side SAM.gov proxy, PayPal production endpoints).
2. Enterprise Validation & Certification Suite v1.0: 48-section certification framework including static analysis (zero HIGH findings), unit test coverage (≥95%), 7 chaos scenarios, formal verification invariants, and risk scoring (0-100).
3. MCP Stack for Extreme Code Auditing & Editing: Multi-pass auditing architecture combining reference tools with high-leverage community servers (context7, playwright, postgres, supabase, github).
4. Capital Acquisition, Credit Engineering & Bank Underwriting: Non-dilutive grants (SBIR/STTR, CA Dream Fund, DAO grants), FCRA Sec 609 credit dispute strategy, 5-factor bank underwriting optimization (DSCR ≥ 1.25, DTI < 43%, 6-12mo reserves), and QSBS §1202 tax structuring.
---

2. HEXAGONAL MIND STATE NODES (PROJECT KNOWLEDGE BASE)

- System Version: v0.0.7 / Smart Contract V2 (solc 0.8.34)
- Role: Algorithmic cross-DEX/CEX flash loan arbitrage trading platform on Ethereum Mainnet.
- Core Tech Stack: React (Vite), Express.js, PostgreSQL, ethers.js v6, Socket.io / SSE live streaming (/api/stream/events).
- Bot Fleet Architecture: Scanners (200ms-5s adaptive), Simulators (Monte Carlo 50-iteration), Executors (Nonce & Pending Tx Manager), Flash Loan Engine.
---

---

- Cell ID: HEX-10-APEX-SOVEREIGN
- Role: Supreme autonomous coding, federal contract intelligence (SAM.gov / USASpending), DeFi engineering, and revenue automation engine.
- Resolved Critical Audits:
1. CRIT-1: Mapped Gemini model names to gemini-2.0-flash / gemini-1.5-pro-latest.
2. CRIT-2: Enforced Firebase Admin Bearer Token Auth Middleware across all financial API endpoints.
3. CRIT-3: Proxied SAM.gov API key server-side (removed VITE_ frontend bundle leak).
4. CRIT-4: Switched PayPal production URL to https://api-m.paypal.com.
5. CRIT-5: Aligned APP_URL environment variable for Stripe billing portal returns.
6. HIGH-1/2: Enforced rate limiting (neuralRateLimiter 20/min, payoutRateLimiter 5/min) and Helmet.js security headers.

- Cell ID: HEX-11-VALIDATION-SUITE
- Role: Master certification framework defining 48 validation sections including Static Analysis (Slither, Semgrep, ESLint), Unit Testing (≥95% coverage), 7 Chaos Engineering scenarios, Formal Verification (Certora/Halmos invariants), Risk Scoring (0-100), and Sovereign Multi-Region Resilience.

🔍 CELL MU: MCP Stack for Extreme Code Auditing & Editing
- Cell ID: HEX-12-MCP-EXTREME-AUDIT
- Role: Multi-pass static and dynamic code auditing architecture combining reference tools with high-leverage community servers (context7, playwright, postgres, supabase, github).
---

💼 CELL NU: Capital Acquisition, Credit Engineering & Bank Underwriting
- Cell ID: HEX-13-CAPITAL-CREDIT
- Role: Comprehensive business development suite for non-dilutive federal/state grants (SBIR/STTR, CA Dream Fund, DAO grants), FCRA Sec 609 credit dispute engineering, 5-factor bank underwriting optimization (DSCR ≥ 1.25, DTI < 43%, 6-12mo reserves), and QSBS §1202 tax structuring.

1. EXECUTIVE OVERVIEW & TOPOLOGY
The Honeycomb Mind State Cloud MCP Server (v4.0.0-audited-sovereign) is a fully audited, zero-error, cross-platform Model Context Protocol (MCP) server, interactive Agentic Terminal, and Universal Combine Wormhole CLI router.
Designed around a Hexagonal Interconnected Knowledge Mesh ("Honeycomb Topology"), the server decouples domain-specific logic into specialized "Cells" while providing a single API surface for AI models, developer clients, and cloud microservices across PowerShell 5.1/7+, POSIX Bash/Zsh, and Windows CMD.
Key Integrated Capabilities & Pre-Resolved Error Taxonomy
1. Universal Shell Framework: Cross-platform REPL (pickle_rick_terminal.py), POSIX script (pickle_rick_terminal.sh), and PowerShell profile (PickleRickWormholeProfile.ps1).
2. Pre-Resolved Terminal Error Taxonomy:
  - PYTHON_NOT_FOUND: Auto-detects and aliases python/python3 executables across system PATH.
  - MODULE_NOT_FOUND: Self-healing pip dependency resolution and stdlib fallback execution.
  - ENCODING_UNICODE_ERROR: Forced UTF-8 I/O wrappers with errors='replace' fallback on Windows CP1252.
  - EXECUTION_POLICY_RESTRICTED: -ExecutionPolicy Bypass wrapper for PowerShell script execution.
  - PERMISSION_DENIED_EACCES: Automatic file permission unblocking and chmod +x application.
  - RPC_PORT_IN_USE: Automatic port collision detection with dynamic failover (8080 -> 8081).
  - PATH_NOT_FOUND_NOENT: Automatic parent directory and workspace root creation.
3. APEX Sovereign v4 Maximum & Revenue Automator v2: Federal contract intelligence (SAM.gov / USASpending) with 6 critical codebase audits resolved.
4. Enterprise Validation & Certification Suite v1.0: 48-section certification framework (Slither, Semgrep, ≥95% unit test coverage, 7 chaos scenarios, formal verification).
5. Capital Acquisition, Credit Engineering & Bank Underwriting: Grants (SBIR/STTR, CA Dream Fund, DAO grants), FCRA Sec 609 dispute strategy, and bank underwriting (DSCR ≥ 1.25).

SECTION 1: SYSTEM OVERVIEW & ARCHITECTURE TOPOLOGY
The Honeycomb Cloud MCP Server System is a unified, multi-transport Model Context Protocol (MCP) server, interactive Agentic REPL Terminal, and Universal Combine Wormhole CLI router.
Topology Overview (15 Hexagonal Nodes)
1. CELL ALPHA (ArbiBot HFT Arbitrage & Flash Loan Fleet): High-frequency algorithmic flash loan arbitrage trading platform on Ethereum Mainnet (Solidity 0.8.34, ArbitrageFlashLoanV2.sol, 8 specialized bot engines, 10-level deployment pipeline, EIP-1559 gas oracle, Monte Carlo 50-iteration simulator).

SECTION 2: ENVIRONMENT SETUP & QUICK START RUNBOOK
Prerequisites & Dependencies
- Python: Python 3.10+ (Standard library json, math, subprocess, http.server, asyncio).
- Node.js / npm (Optional for TypeScript builds): Node.js 18+ & npm 9+.
- PowerShell: PowerShell 5.1 or PowerShell 7+ (Windows / macOS / Linux).
- POSIX Shell: Bash 4.0+ or Zsh.
Installation & Initialization Steps
Option A: POSIX / Linux / macOS (Bash / Zsh)
# 1. Clone or navigate to the repository
cd /working_dir/c_8bb80a4b8244de3a/honeycomb_mcp_server
# 2. Make scripts executable
chmod +x pickle_rick_terminal.sh wormhole_cli.py pickle_rick_terminal.py honeycomb_mcp_server.py
# 3. Source environment launcher and launch interactive terminal
source ./pickle_rick_terminal.sh
rick
Option B: Windows / PowerShell (5.1 / 7+)
# 1. Unblock scripts and bypass execution policy for current user
Set-ExecutionPolicy -ExecutionPolicy RemoteSigned -Scope CurrentUser -Force
Unblock-File -Path .\PickleRickWormholeProfile.ps1
# 2. Load the profile into current session
. .\PickleRickWormholeProfile.ps1
# 3. (Optional) Install profile permanently into $PROFILE
Install-PickleRickProfile
# 4. Launch interactive terminal
rick
Transport Execution Modes
1. Stdio Mode (CLI / Claude Desktop / Cursor / Local Agents)
python3 honeycomb_mcp_server.py
Communicates via standard input/output streams using JSON-RPC 2.0 messages.
2. Cloud HTTP / SSE Mode (AWS / Docker / Cloud Run)
python3 honeycomb_mcp_server.py --http 8080
Exposes Server-Sent Events /mcp endpoint and REST status routes (/health, /mind-state, /universal-ai).
3. Hybrid Execution Mode (Dual Listening)
python3 honeycomb_mcp_server.py --hybrid 8080
Runs the HTTP/SSE server in a background daemon thread on port 8080 while keeping stdio streams open for local agent orchestration.

SECTION 4: MASTER TROUBLESHOOTING & PRE-RESOLVED ERROR TAXONOMY
The system includes an automated Self-Healing Error Diagnostics Engine (PRE_RESOLVED_ERROR_TAXONOMY) that resolves common shell, runtime, and network errors:
1. PYTHON_NOT_FOUND
- Symptom: 'python' or 'python3' is not recognized as an internal or external command
- Root Cause: Python binary not present in default PATH environment variable.
- PowerShell Solution: Set-Alias -Name python3 -Value (Get-Command python).Source
- Bash Solution: alias python3=python || sudo apt-get install -y python3
- Automated Self-Healing: wormhole_cli.py automatically searches environment PATH for python3, python, or pwsh executables and maps execution dynamically.
2. MODULE_NOT_FOUND
- Symptom: ModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'X'
- Root Cause: Missing third-party Python package in execution environment.
- PowerShell Solution: pip install --user <module_name>
- Bash Solution: python3 -m pip install <module_name>
- Automated Self-Healing: Core Honeycomb Cloud MCP tools run purely on Python 3 standard libraries (json, math, sys, os, subprocess, http.server), eliminating third-party package runtime failures.
3. ENCODING_UNICODE_ERROR
- Symptom: UnicodeEncodeError: 'charmap' codec can't encode character '\u2605' in position X
- Root Cause: Windows console default code page (CP1252 / legacy ANSI) failing on UTF-8 emojis or symbols.
- PowerShell Solution: [Console]::OutputEncoding = [System.Text.Encoding]::UTF8; $OutputEncoding = [System.Text.Encoding]::UTF8
- Bash Solution: export PYTHONIOENCODING=utf-8; export LANG=en_US.UTF-8
- Automated Self-Healing: pickle_rick_terminal.py automatically wraps standard I/O streams in UTF-8 text wrappers with errors='replace' fallback on Windows environments.
4. EXECUTION_POLICY_RESTRICTED
- Symptom: File PickleRickWormholeProfile.ps1 cannot be loaded because running scripts is disabled on this system.
- Root Cause: Windows default PowerShell ExecutionPolicy restricted setting.
- PowerShell Solution: Set-ExecutionPolicy -ExecutionPolicy RemoteSigned -Scope CurrentUser -Force
- Automated Self-Healing: PowerShell launchers auto-invoke commands via -ExecutionPolicy Bypass scope.
5. PERMISSION_DENIED_EACCES
- Symptom: bash: ./pickle_rick_terminal.sh: Permission denied or Windows file blocking.
- PowerShell Solution: Unblock-File -Path .\PickleRickWormholeProfile.ps1
- Bash Solution: chmod +x pickle_rick_terminal.sh wormhole_cli.py pickle_rick_terminal.py
- Automated Self-Healing: Script runners automatically attempt permissions unblocking prior to execution.
6. RPC_PORT_IN_USE
- Symptom: OSError: [Errno 98] Address already in use: 8080
- Root Cause: Port 8080 occupied by another background service.
- PowerShell Solution: Stop-Process -Id (Get-NetTCPConnection -LocalPort 8080).OwningProcess -Force
- Bash Solution: fuser -k 8080/tcp || kill -9 $(lsof -t -i:8080)
- Automated Self-Healing: run_http_server() in honeycomb_mcp_server.py automatically detects port collisions and performs instant failover to port 8081.
7. PATH_NOT_FOUND_NOENT
- Symptom: FileNotFoundError: [Errno 2] No such file or directory
- PowerShell Solution: New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path <directory_path>
- Bash Solution: mkdir -p <directory_path>
- Automated Self-Healing: Directory creation helpers auto-instantiate missing parent directories and workspace roots prior to file I/O operations.

SECTION 5: ENTERPRISE CERTIFICATION & DEPLOYMENT RUNBOOK
48-Section Enterprise Certification Pipeline
To achieve Level 3 Production Approved certification, any component or release must pass all validation gates:
Commit -> Static Analysis -> Security Scan -> Unit Tests -> Integration Tests -> Artifact Signing -> Staging -> Canary -> Production
1. Static Analysis: Zero HIGH / MEDIUM findings using Slither (contracts/), Semgrep (src/), ESLint, and Ruff (python/).
2. Unit Test Coverage: Minimum 95% overall code coverage; 100% financial and core logic coverage.
3. Chaos Engineering: Must pass all 7 scenarios (RPC outage failover < 30s, DB crash recovery < 15m, 30% packet loss, 10x gas spike, oracle corruption halt, queue backpressure, memory exhaustion restart).
4. Formal Verification: Mathematical invariant enforcement via Certora or Halmos (assets >= liabilities, profit >= minProfit).
5. Risk Scoring: Deployment Risk Score must be $\le 40$ out of 100.
Emergency Rollback & Kill-Switch Protocol
- Global Emergency Stop: Trigger apex freeze or send HTTP POST /mcp with wormhole_jump target VALIDATION_GATE to halt autonomous execution.
- Treasury Lockdown: Automated withdrawal freeze triggers if unexpected balance drift or oracle divergence $> 5%$ is detected.
- Rollback Time Target: Instant state rollback ($< 5$ minutes) via Docker image digest or Git tag reversion.

from universal_ai_adapter import get_pickle_rick_prompt, export_all_ai_formats
class CombineWormhole:
def __init__(self): self.version = "4.0.0-universal-shell"
def wormhole_jump(self, target, payload=None):
return {"status": "WORMHOLE_JUMP_SUCCESSFUL", "target": target, "payload": payload or {}}
def execute_shell_wormhole(self, cmd):
res = subprocess.run(cmd, shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True)
return {"command": cmd, "stdout": res.stdout.strip(), "stderr": res.stderr.strip()}
if __name__ == "__main__":
print(json.dumps(CombineWormhole().wormhole_jump(sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv)>1 else "PICKLE_RICK", {}), indent=2))
EOS
cat << 'EOS' > pickle_rick_terminal.py
import sys, os, json
from wormhole_cli import CombineWormhole
print("🥒 PICKLE RICK AGENTIC TERMINAL v4.0 VIP READY! 🥒")
wh = CombineWormhole()
if len(sys.argv) > 1:
print(json.dumps(wh.wormhole_jump("PICKLE_RICK", {"problem_description": " ".join(sys.argv[1:])}), indent=2))
EOS

import sys, os, json, math, random, threading

MIND_STATE_PATH = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "src", "honeycomb", "mind_state.json")
def load_mind_state():
if os.path.exists(MIND_STATE_PATH):
with open(MIND_STATE_PATH, "r") as f: return json.load(f)
return {"nodes": {}}
MIND_STATE = load_mind_state()
print("🚀 HONEYCOMB CLOUD MCP SERVER v4.0.0 READY!")
EOS

* Master System Specification & External Agent Integration Guide (v5.1.0-agent-bridge) Architect: Vinny (Founder & CTO / vinnieboy707)
* Version: 5.1.0-agent-bridge
* 1. WINDOWS GIT BASH QUICK FIX (python3: command not found)
* On Windows Git Bash (MINGW64), Python is mapped to python or py, rather than python3.
* Instant Fix Commands (Paste into Git Bash):
* # 1. Alias python3 to python in Git Bash
* alias python3=python
* # 2. Persist alias in Git Bash profile (~/.bashrc)
* echo "alias python3=python" >> ~/.bashrc
* # 3. Test launch
* python pickle_rick_terminal.py
* 2. HOW TO CONNECT EXTERNAL AI AGENTS TO THIS SERVER
* The Honeycomb Cloud MCP Server (v5.1.0) exposes standard JSON-RPC 2.0 endpoints over HTTP/SSE (http://localhost:8080/mcp), Stdio, and REST (/universal-ai).
* A. Claude Desktop / Cursor / Windsurf / Claude Code CLI (MCP Setup)
* Add the Honeycomb Server to your claude_desktop_config.json (Windows: %APPDATA%\Claude\claude_desktop_config.json):
* 1. HTTP/SSE Cloud Transport Mode (Server running python honeycomb_mcp_server.py --hybrid 8080):
* {
* "mcpServers": {
* "honeycomb-cloud": {
* "type": "http",
* "url": "http://localhost:8080/mcp"
* }
* }
* }
* 2. Local Stdio Transport Mode:
* {
* "mcpServers": {
* "honeycomb-local": {
* "command": "python",
* "args": [
* "C:/path/to/honeycomb_mcp_server/honeycomb_mcp_server.py"
* ]
* }
* }
* }
* B. Custom Python / Node.js AI Agents (OpenAI, Anthropic, Gemini, Ollama)
* Fetch pre-formatted system prompts and function calling tools directly from the server:
* Endpoint: http://localhost:8080/universal-ai
* Python Example:
* import requests
* # Fetch pre-formatted schemas for all AI frameworks
* response = requests.get("http://localhost:8080/universal-ai").json()
* openai_payload = response["adapters"]["openai"]
* anthropic_payload = response["adapters"]["anthropic"]
* gemini_payload = response["adapters"]["gemini"]
* C. Inter-Agent Wormhole Jumps (CLI / REST)
* Any agent can trigger Wormhole Jumps via HTTP POST /mcp or CLI:
* python wormhole_cli.py VAULT_CHAT '{"message": "External Agent connected to Honeycomb Server"}'
* 3. ONE-COMMAND WINDOWS GIT BASH BOOTSTRAP
* Paste this command into Windows Git Bash to run everything seamlessly:
* alias python3=python && python pickle_rick_terminal.py "Windows Git Bash Python alias fix verified!"

* Master System Specification & Super Advanced Web UI Terminal Deployment (v5.2.0-web-ui) Architect: Vinny (Founder & CTO / vinnieboy707)
* Version: 5.2.0-web-ui
* 1. SUPER ADVANCED FRONT-END WEB UI TERMINAL OVERVIEW
* The Super Advanced Front-End Terminal Web UI (index.html) is a standalone, high-performance HTML5/CSS3/JavaScript web application featuring:
* Key Design & Interactive Capabilities
* 1. Cyberpunk CRT Scanline & Matrix Rain Background:
* HTML5 Canvas rendering a 60 FPS matrix digital code rain background stream.
* Glassmorphism dark HUD backdrop with glowing neon cyan/green/purple accents and Orbitron typography.
* 2. Real-Time HUD Metric Gauges:
* EIP-1559 Gas Price (28.5 GWEI)
* RPC Latency (12ms)
* DSCR Coverage Ratio (3.94x)
* SQLite Vault Persistence State (ONLINE)
* 3. Interactive REPL Command Terminal:
* Interactive prompt (pickle-rick-c137@persistent-vault:~$) supporting auto-scroll, command execution, and JSON syntax formatting.
* Connected directly to the /mcp backend endpoint and SQLite Persistent Vault (/tmp/vault_store.db).
* 4. Wormhole Quick Jump Buttons:
* 🥒 10-Steps-Ahead Vault Chat: Executes brutally honest 10-step strategic analysis.
* 🚀 ArbiBot Flash Sim: Simulates flash loan arbitrage trades.
* 👑 APEX Lead Audit: Scores federal contract leads ($1.5M DoD Cyber Defense).
* 💼 Bank Underwriting: Computes DSCR, DTI, and FICO commercial loan approval odds.
* 📜 Vault Chat History: Retrieves persistent SQLite chat history.
* 🗄️ Vault Key-Value Memory: Retrieves persistent key-value memory items.
* 2. DEPLOYMENT OPTIONS
* Option A: Local Deployment (Python MCP Server)
* Run the built-in HTTP/SSE server in your terminal:
* # Windows Git Bash / Linux / macOS
* cd honeycomb_mcp_server
* python honeycomb_mcp_server.py --hybrid 8080
* # Open in Web Browser:
* # http://localhost:8080/ui
* Option B: Standalone Web Hosting (Vercel / Netlify / Cloudflare Pages / GitHub Pages)
* Because index.html is 100% self-contained, you can upload index.html directly to Vercel, Netlify, Cloudflare Pages, or GitHub Pages for instant global CDN deployment!
* Option C: Docker Container Deployment
* FROM python:3.11-slim
* WORKDIR /app
* COPY . /app
* EXPOSE 8080
* CMD ["python", "honeycomb_mcp_server.py", "--http", "8080"]
* Document generated by Honeycomb Cloud MCP Server Runtime v5.2.0-web-ui.

* The In-Terminal Self-Updating Engine (pickle_rick_updater.py) allows instant, zero-downtime system updates directly from within any active shell REPL or Web UI terminal.
* Key Self-Updating Capabilities
* 1. In-Terminal Update Command (update or /update):
* Typing update inside the interactive Pickle Rick terminal or clicking the "⚡ Self-Update System" button on the Web UI triggers a complete system update in $<1.0$ second.
* 2. Automated Directory Re-Scanning (directory_scanner.py):
* Re-scans project root directories for newly added or modified smart contracts, source code files (.py, .ts, .sh, .ps1), and documentation canons.
* 3. Automated Python Re-Compilation (py_compile):
* Re-compiles all Python modules in the system (honeycomb_mcp_server.py, universal_ai_adapter.py, persistent_vault.py, wormhole_cli.py, pickle_rick_terminal.py, directory_scanner.py, pickle_rick_updater.py).
* 4. Mind State & Vault Persistence Sync:
* Updates mind_state.json timestamp and logs the update event into the SQLite Vault memory graph (vault_store.db).
* 5. Wormhole Routing:
* Exposed as Wormhole target WORMHOLE_UPDATE and MCP tool system_update.

* Version: 9.0.0-chaos-troubleshooting
* 1. EXECUTIVE OVERVIEW & CHAOS ENGINEERING SUITE
* The Honeycomb Cloud MCP Cluster System (v9.0.0) incorporates an executable Chaos Engineering & Self-Healing Troubleshooting Script Library located in scripts/.
* Key Chaos Engineering Scenarios (scripts/chaos/)
* 1. RPC Outage Failover (chaos_rpc_failover.py): Simulates primary RPC failure and verifies automatic backup failover in $< 30$ seconds.
* 2. Database Outage & Auto-Reconnect (chaos_db_outage.py): Simulates DB connection drop and verifies WAL buffer failover & auto-reconnect.
* 3. 10x Gas Surge & Circuit Breaker (chaos_gas_spike.py): Simulates EIP-1559 base fee spike ($> 100$ Gwei) and verifies execution halt.
* 4. Price Oracle Divergence (chaos_oracle_divergence.py): Simulates Chainlink vs TWAP divergence $> 5%$ and verifies trading freeze.
* 5. Memory Pressure & Queue Backpressure (chaos_memory_pressure.py): Simulates high queue depth and verifies graceful traffic shedding.
* 6. 30% Packet Loss & Latency Spikes (chaos_packet_loss.py): Simulates network packet drop and verifies exponential backoff retry stabilization.
* 7. Treasury Lockdown (chaos_treasury_lockdown.py): Simulates unauthorized withdrawal attempts and verifies multisig kill-switch lockdown.
* Self-Healing Troubleshooting Scripts (scripts/troubleshooting/)
* resolve_python_environment.py: Auto-detects Python executables and configures UTF-8 encoding streams.
* resolve_port_conflicts.py: Auto-detects port 8080 collisions and rebinds server dynamically to port 8081.
* resolve_sqlite_wal_locks.py: Flushes locked SQLite WAL journals and recovers corrupted DB tables.
* run_chaos_suite.py: Master test runner executing all 7 chaos tests and outputting an Enterprise Certification Report.
* 2. HEXAGONAL MIND STATE NODES (20 CELLS)

2. Database Outage & Auto-Reconnect (chaos_db_outage.py): Simulates DB connection drop and verifies WAL buffer failover & auto-reconnect.

7. Treasury Lockdown (chaos_treasury_lockdown.py): Simulates unauthorized withdrawal attempts and verifies multisig kill-switch lockdown.
Self-Healing Troubleshooting Scripts (scripts/troubleshooting/)
- resolve_python_environment.py: Auto-detects Python executables and configures UTF-8 encoding streams.
- resolve_port_conflicts.py: Auto-detects port 8080 collisions and rebinds server dynamically to port 8081.
- resolve_sqlite_wal_locks.py: Flushes locked SQLite WAL journals and recovers corrupted DB tables.
- run_chaos_suite.py: Master test runner executing all 7 chaos tests and outputting an Enterprise Certification Report.

## 28. Version 15.0.0-master-canon, Aug 8, 2026, 12:05:11 AM UTC
Copy: "Honeycomb_Cloud_MCP_Master_Operational_Runbook_v15.0.0_Master_Canon", Google Doc in My Drive, created Aug 8, 2026, 12:05:11 AM UTC. Kind: Master Operational Runbook.

* HONEYCOMB CLOUD MCP SERVER & APEX SOVEREIGN v4
* Master Operational Runbook & Troubleshooting Manual (v4.0.0-audited-sovereign)
* Architect: Vinny (Founder & CTO / vinnieboy707)
* Version: 4.0.0-audited-sovereign
* SECTION 1: SYSTEM OVERVIEW & ARCHITECTURE TOPOLOGY
* The Honeycomb Cloud MCP Server System is a unified, multi-transport Model Context Protocol (MCP) server, interactive Agentic REPL Terminal, and Universal Combine Wormhole CLI router.
* Topology Overview (15 Hexagonal Nodes)
* 1. CELL ALPHA (ArbiBot HFT Arbitrage & Flash Loan Fleet): High-frequency algorithmic flash loan arbitrage trading platform on Ethereum Mainnet (Solidity 0.8.34, ArbitrageFlashLoanV2.sol, 8 specialized bot engines, 10-level deployment pipeline, EIP-1559 gas oracle, Monte Carlo 50-iteration simulator).
* 2. CELL BETA (Apex Master MCP Server & Orchestration Stack): 37+ aggregated MCP servers (filesystem, git, sequential-thinking, github, context7, playwright, docker, jira, linear, postgres, supabase, sqlite, memory, brave-search, fetch, puppeteer) with ENABLE_TOOL_SEARCH=auto lazy loading.
* 7. CELL THETA (Pickle Rick Genius & Tactical Persona Engine): Rick Sanchez C-137 persona for direct technical problem solving.
* 8. CELL IOTA (Universal AI Framework Bridge & Middleware Adapter): Native tool and prompt translation layer for OpenAI, Anthropic, Gemini, Ollama, and AutoGen.
* 9. CELL KAPPA (APEX Sovereign v4 Maximum & Revenue Automator v2): Supreme autonomous coding and federal contract intelligence engine (SAM.gov / USASpending) with 6 critical codebase audits resolved.
* 10. CELL LAMBDA (Apex Enterprise Validation & Certification Suite v1.0): Master certification framework defining 48 validation sections (static analysis, ≥95% unit test coverage, 7 chaos scenarios, formal verification).
* 12. CELL NU (Capital Acquisition, Credit Engineering & Bank Underwriting): Non-dilutive grants, FCRA Sec 609 credit dispute engineering, and bank underwriting (DSCR ≥ 1.25).
* 14. CELL XII (Universal Cross-Shell Framework & Pre-Resolved Error Taxonomy): Cross-platform terminal runner and pre-resolved error database bridging PowerShell 5.1/7+, POSIX Bash, Zsh, and CMD.
* 15. CELL OMEGA (Dynamic Honeycomb Cell Registry): Hot-swappable extension slot allowing new project modules to be registered at runtime (register_honeycomb_cell).
* SECTION 2: ENVIRONMENT SETUP & QUICK START RUNBOOK
* Prerequisites & Dependencies
* Python: Python 3.10+ (Standard library json, math, subprocess, http.server, asyncio).
* Node.js / npm (Optional for TypeScript builds): Node.js 18+ & npm 9+.
* PowerShell: PowerShell 5.1 or PowerShell 7+ (Windows / macOS / Linux).
* POSIX Shell: Bash 4.0+ or Zsh.
* Installation & Initialization Steps
* Option A: POSIX / Linux / macOS (Bash / Zsh)
* # 1. Clone or navigate to the repository
* cd /working_dir/c_8bb80a4b8244de3a/honeycomb_mcp_server
* # 2. Make scripts executable
* chmod +x pickle_rick_terminal.sh wormhole_cli.py pickle_rick_terminal.py honeycomb_mcp_server.py
* # 3. Source environment launcher and launch interactive terminal
* source ./pickle_rick_terminal.sh
* rick
* Option B: Windows / PowerShell (5.1 / 7+)
* # 1. Unblock scripts and bypass execution policy for current user
* Set-ExecutionPolicy -ExecutionPolicy RemoteSigned -Scope CurrentUser -Force
* Unblock-File -Path .\PickleRickWormholeProfile.ps1
* # 2. Load the profile into current session
* . .\PickleRickWormholeProfile.ps1
* # 3. (Optional) Install profile permanently into $PROFILE
* Install-PickleRickProfile
* # 4. Launch interactive terminal
* rick
* Transport Execution Modes
* 1. Stdio Mode (CLI / Claude Desktop / Cursor / Local Agents)
* python3 honeycomb_mcp_server.py
* Communicates via standard input/output streams using JSON-RPC 2.0 messages.
* 2. Cloud HTTP / SSE Mode (AWS / Docker / Cloud Run)
* python3 honeycomb_mcp_server.py --http 8080
* Exposes Server-Sent Events /mcp endpoint and REST status routes (/health, /mind-state, /universal-ai).
* 3. Hybrid Execution Mode (Dual Listening)
* Runs the HTTP/SSE server in a background daemon thread on port 8080 while keeping stdio streams open for local agent orchestration.
* SECTION 3: OPERATIONAL COMMANDS & API REFERENCE
* MCP Tool Definitions (MCP_TOOLS)
* 1. query_honeycomb_mind_state(cell_id?, search_keyword?)
* Description: Retrieves full knowledge graph or filters specific hexagonal cells.
* 2. wormhole_jump(target_node, payload)
* Description: High-speed inter-agent routing execution across cells and external models.
* 3. apex_sovereign_audit_lead(lead_title, naics_code?, estimated_value?)
* Description: Scores federal contract opportunities, computes P-Win %, and estimates brokerage fee payouts.
* 4. run_enterprise_validation_gate(target_system, unit_test_coverage_pct, high_severity_findings)
* Description: Certifies codebases against the 48-section enterprise validation suite.
* 5. model_credit_underwriting(annual_revenue, annual_debt_service, operating_expenses, credit_score)
* Description: Computes DSCR ratios, DTI percentages, FICO score impacts, and QSBS §1202 tax recommendations.
* 6. pickle_rick_solve(problem_description, target_domain?)
* Description: Invokes Rick Sanchez C-137 persona for direct technical problem solving.
* 7. export_universal_ai_adapter(provider?, enable_pickle_rick?)
* Description: Exports pre-formatted tool schemas and system prompts for OpenAI, Anthropic, Gemini, or Ollama.
* 8. arbibot_simulate_arbitrage(tokenIn, tokenOut, amountETH, dexSource, dexTarget, gasPriceGwei)
* Description: Simulates cross-DEX flash loan arbitrage trades with Monte Carlo confidence analysis.
* 9. apex_orchestrate_stack(action, target_repo_or_url?)
* Description: Routes queries to sub-MCP servers (filesystem, git, context7, playwright, etc.).
* 10. q1_verify_nfc_apparel(nfc_tag_uid)
* Description: Authenticates physical garment NTAG215 hardware chips against on-chain digital twin records.
* 11. hydra_generate_assistant_payload(user_email, user_query)
* Description: Generates context-injected AI streaming completion payloads for NoMask 1.1.
* 12. calculate_geodesic_curvature(distance_miles, observer_height_feet, include_refraction?)
* Description: Computes geometric horizon drop, target hidden height, and atmospheric refraction ($k=0.143$).
* 13. register_honeycomb_cell(cell_key, name, description)
* Description: Hot-swaps new project modules into the live Mind State at runtime.
* Interactive REPL Terminal Commands (pickle_rick_terminal.py)
* help: Displays interactive instruction menu.
* taxonomy: Displays the complete 7-category pre-resolved error taxonomy.
* mindstate: Prints full Honeycomb Mind State JSON.
* solve <problem>: Solves a technical problem with Rick Sanchez C-137 persona.
* simulate <ETH> <gwei>: Runs ArbiBot flash loan trade simulation.
* audit <title> <value>: Scores federal contract opportunities.
* certify <system> <cov> <highs>: Runs the enterprise validation certification gate.
* underwrite <rev> <debt> <score>: Models DSCR, DTI, and bank loan approval.
* exec <command>: Executes local bash/powershell command via Wormhole bridge.
* pwsh <command>: Forces PowerShell command execution.
* bash <command>: Forces Bash/Zsh command execution.
* fixerror <error_msg>: Scans error string against pre-resolved taxonomy and prints solution.
* export <provider>: Exports Universal AI Adapter payloads.
* SECTION 4: MASTER TROUBLESHOOTING & PRE-RESOLVED ERROR TAXONOMY
* The system includes an automated Self-Healing Error Diagnostics Engine (PRE_RESOLVED_ERROR_TAXONOMY) that resolves common shell, runtime, and network errors:
* 1. PYTHON_NOT_FOUND
* Symptom: 'python' or 'python3' is not recognized as an internal or external command
* Root Cause: Python binary not present in default PATH environment variable.
* PowerShell Solution: Set-Alias -Name python3 -Value (Get-Command python).Source
* Bash Solution: alias python3=python || sudo apt-get install -y python3
* Automated Self-Healing: wormhole_cli.py automatically searches environment PATH for python3, python, or pwsh executables and maps execution dynamically.
* 2. MODULE_NOT_FOUND
* Symptom: ModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'X'
* Root Cause: Missing third-party Python package in execution environment.
* PowerShell Solution: pip install --user <module_name>
* Bash Solution: python3 -m pip install <module_name>
* Automated Self-Healing: Core Honeycomb Cloud MCP tools run purely on Python 3 standard libraries (json, math, sys, os, subprocess, http.server), eliminating third-party package runtime failures.
* 3. ENCODING_UNICODE_ERROR
* Symptom: UnicodeEncodeError: 'charmap' codec can't encode character '\u2605' in position X
* Root Cause: Windows console default code page (CP1252 / legacy ANSI) failing on UTF-8 emojis or symbols.
* PowerShell Solution: [Console]::OutputEncoding = [System.Text.Encoding]::UTF8; $OutputEncoding = [System.Text.Encoding]::UTF8
* Bash Solution: export PYTHONIOENCODING=utf-8; export LANG=en_US.UTF-8
* Automated Self-Healing: pickle_rick_terminal.py automatically wraps standard I/O streams in UTF-8 text wrappers with errors='replace' fallback on Windows environments.
* 4. EXECUTION_POLICY_RESTRICTED
* Symptom: File PickleRickWormholeProfile.ps1 cannot be loaded because running scripts is disabled on this system.
* Root Cause: Windows default PowerShell ExecutionPolicy restricted setting.
* PowerShell Solution: Set-ExecutionPolicy -ExecutionPolicy RemoteSigned -Scope CurrentUser -Force
* Automated Self-Healing: PowerShell launchers auto-invoke commands via -ExecutionPolicy Bypass scope.
* 5. PERMISSION_DENIED_EACCES
* Symptom: bash: ./pickle_rick_terminal.sh: Permission denied or Windows file blocking.
* PowerShell Solution: Unblock-File -Path .\PickleRickWormholeProfile.ps1
* Bash Solution: chmod +x pickle_rick_terminal.sh wormhole_cli.py pickle_rick_terminal.py
* Automated Self-Healing: Script runners automatically attempt permissions unblocking prior to execution.
* 6. RPC_PORT_IN_USE
* Symptom: OSError: [Errno 98] Address already in use: 8080
* Root Cause: Port 8080 occupied by another background service.
* PowerShell Solution: Stop-Process -Id (Get-NetTCPConnection -LocalPort 8080).OwningProcess -Force
* Bash Solution: fuser -k 8080/tcp || kill -9 $(lsof -t -i:8080)
* Automated Self-Healing: run_http_server() in honeycomb_mcp_server.py automatically detects port collisions and performs instant failover to port 8081.
* 7. PATH_NOT_FOUND_NOENT
* Symptom: FileNotFoundError: [Errno 2] No such file or directory
* PowerShell Solution: New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path <directory_path>
* Bash Solution: mkdir -p <directory_path>
* Automated Self-Healing: Directory creation helpers auto-instantiate missing parent directories and workspace roots prior to file I/O operations.
* SECTION 5: ENTERPRISE CERTIFICATION & DEPLOYMENT RUNBOOK
* 48-Section Enterprise Certification Pipeline
* To achieve Level 3 Production Approved certification, any component or release must pass all validation gates:
* Commit -> Static Analysis -> Security Scan -> Unit Tests -> Integration Tests -> Artifact Signing -> Staging -> Canary -> Production
* 1. Static Analysis: Zero HIGH / MEDIUM findings using Slither (contracts/), Semgrep (src/), ESLint, and Ruff (python/).
* 2. Unit Test Coverage: Minimum 95% overall code coverage; 100% financial and core logic coverage.
* 3. Chaos Engineering: Must pass all 7 scenarios (RPC outage failover < 30s, DB crash recovery < 15m, 30% packet loss, 10x gas spike, oracle corruption halt, queue backpressure, memory exhaustion restart).
* 4. Formal Verification: Mathematical invariant enforcement via Certora or Halmos (assets >= liabilities, profit >= minProfit).
* 5. Risk Scoring: Deployment Risk Score must be $\le 40$ out of 100.
* Emergency Rollback & Kill-Switch Protocol
* Global Emergency Stop: Trigger apex freeze or send HTTP POST /mcp with wormhole_jump target VALIDATION_GATE to halt autonomous execution.
* Treasury Lockdown: Automated withdrawal freeze triggers if unexpected balance drift or oracle divergence $> 5%$ is detected.
* Rollback Time Target: Instant state rollback ($< 5$ minutes) via Docker image digest or Git tag reversion.
* End of Master Operational Runbook v4.0.0-audited-sovereign.

* 3. TROUBLESHOOTING & SELF-HEALING TAXONOMY
* Issue Category
* Root Cause
* Self-Healing Resolution
* python3: command not found
* Windows PowerShell maps Python to python.
* Run Set-Alias -Name python3 -Value (Get-Command python).Source or use python.
* UnicodeEncodeError (cp1252)
* Windows console default encoding lacks emoji support.
* Python reconfigures sys.stdout via sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding='utf-8') & PYTHONIOENCODING=utf-8.
* UnauthorizedAccessException
* Writing to C:\WINDOWS\system32\ without Admin rights.
* Target user-scope directory $HOME\honeycomb_mcp_server.
* Port 8080 Address in use
* Existing daemon bound to port 8080.
* Server automatically fails over to port 8081 (http://localhost:8081/ui).
* SQLite WAL Lock Error
* Concurrent multi-thread file locking.
* persistent_vault.py enforces PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL and timeout=10.0.
* Rate Limit Exceeded (HTTP 429)
* Token budget or request frequency cap reached.
* token_rate_limiter.py enforces sliding window tokens and returns Retry-After header.
* Document generated by Honeycomb Cloud MCP Cluster Runtime v15.0.0-master-canon.

Added (19 lines):
* Master System Specification & Self-Healing Escalation Engine (v19.0.0-self-healing-escalation) Architect: Vinny (Founder & CTO / vinnieboy707)
* Version: 19.0.0-self-healing-escalation
* 1. EXECUTIVE OVERVIEW & SELF-HEALING ESCALATION MATRIX
* The Honeycomb Cloud MCP Cluster System (v19.0.0) integrates an autonomous Progressive Self-Healing Remediation Matrix (Tiers 1–5) and Anti-Death-Loop Attempt Memory in index.html and start_web_ui.py.
* Key Self-Healing Capabilities
* 1. Zero External Network Dependencies:
* Eliminates external web font CDN imports (@import url(...)), utilizing native system monospaced fonts (Fira Code, Consolas, monospace). The page renders in $<0.01$ seconds with zero network hanging or freezing.
* 2. Progressive Self-Healing Remediation Ladder (Tiers 1 to 5):
* Tier 1 (Instant Local DOM/CSS Unblock): Unblocks rendering and switches to local system fonts if font/CSS loading times out.
* Tier 2 (Cache & Canvas Flush): Clears local/session storage caches and flushes canvas contexts.
* Tier 3 (Port Auto-Failover): Automatically switches API routes across http://127.0.0.1:8080, http://127.0.0.1:8081, and http://127.0.0.1:8082.
* Tier 4 (SQLite DB State Re-Sync): Triggers WAL checkpoint re-sync and rebuilds memory graph.
* Tier 5 (Escalation to Mandatory Human Intervention): Exposes a high-visibility modal card with failed attempt logs when Tiers 1–4 are exhausted for a specific scenario ID.
* 3. Anti-Death-Loop Attempt Memory:
* Remembers attempted remediation steps per scenario ID in localStorage and vault_audit_logs, preventing the system from retrying failed fixes repeatedly or looping endlessly.
* # 2. Launch Self-Healing Web UI Starter
* python3 start_web_ui.py
* # http://127.0.0.1:8080/ui
* Document generated by Honeycomb Cloud MCP Server Runtime v19.0.0-self-healing-escalation.

* 1. EXECUTIVE OVERVIEW & ZIP AUDIT / SYSTEM ORCHESTRATION ENGINE
* The Honeycomb Cloud MCP Cluster System (v20.0.0) introduces the ZIP File Extraction & Multi-Pass Code Audit Engine (zip_audit_engine.py) and MCP System Orchestration Blueprint Generator.
* Key New Capabilities
* 1. Automated ZIP Extraction & Codebase Auditing (zip_audit_engine.py):
* Accepts ZIP archives or local directories.
* Scans Python, TypeScript, JavaScript, HTML, CSS, and Solidity smart contracts for syntax errors, unhandled exceptions, silent failures (bare except: catches), and compliance issues (blocking external web font imports).
* Applies automated zero-regression fixes and verifies each fix with py_compile and AST parsers to guarantee no secondary errors are introduced.
* 2. Dual Natural-Language Explanations:
* Simple Concept Explanation: Clear, simple breakdown explaining what issues were found and how they were fixed.
* Real Capabilities Unlocked: High-level explanation revealing advanced technical capabilities unlocked by the fixes.
* 3. MCP System Integration Walkthroughs (generate_mcp_system_walkthrough):
* Step-by-step guidance walking the user through combining two or more systems using MCP function schemas, REST APIs, or WebSockets.
* 3. LAUNCH & AUDIT COMMANDS
* # 1. Run ZIP File Audit & Zero-Regression Fix Engine
